# Current Project State (`CURRENT_STATE.md`)

> **Primary Agent Starting Point.** Last verified: **2026-11-20** (Total Asset Turnover Calculator live at `/tools/total-asset-turnover-calculator`; Auto Loan remains master Tool Detail Page).

---

## 1. Snapshot Summary

- **Repository Setup:** Independent `frontend/` (Next.js 15 App Router, React 19, Tailwind CSS) and `backend/` (Express 4, Mongoose/MongoDB, TypeScript) applications with no npm workspaces.
- **Brand:** Codebase refactored from **Mega Tools** to **Omega Calculators** (`136359a`); some legacy `mega_tools` / `megatools.io` strings remain in env defaults and mock/placeholder files.
- **Core Rule:** `Browser → Next.js Server → Express API → MongoDB`. Media storage follows `STORAGE_DRIVER`: `local` (default, files in `backend/uploads`) or `s3` (direct browser `PUT` to a signed URL).

---

## 2. Feature Status Matrix

### Implemented (Verified End-to-End)
- **Total Asset Turnover Calculator (`/tools/total-asset-turnover-calculator`):** Live client-side TAT tool (`componentKey: 'TotalAssetTurnoverCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e990144`, `nameKey` `total asset turnover calculator`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): Average asset (beginning / ending → average) + Total asset turnover (revenue → TAT×); empty ≠ zero; zero average → unavailable (no Infinity); zero revenue → 0.00x; expandable breakdown. Denomination-only CurrencySelect (PKR default, no FX). No intensity / reverse-mode UI (ADR-023). Activated; **`indexable` left false**. Example: Beg **$8M** · End **$10M** · Rev **$10M** → Avg **$9M** · TAT ≈ **1.11x**.
- **HELOC Calculator (`/tools/heloc-calculator`):** Live client-side Home Equity Line of Credit tool (`componentKey: 'HelocCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e99014f`, `nameKey` `heloc calculator`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): HELOC details (balance / draw years / repayment years / rate / upfront % + fixed / annual fee) + interest-rate adjustments (none | percentage-point schedule with first adjustment, interval months, lifetime cap); interest-only draw → amortizing repayment with payment recalculation on rate change; month schedule + SVG balance/interest/principal chart; fees not financed into principal; annual fee on months 12,24,…. Denomination-only CurrencySelect (PKR default, no FX). Activated; **`indexable` left false**. Example: **50,000** @ **6%** · draw **10y** · repay **20y** → draw **250**/mo · repay ≈ **358.22**/mo · draw interest **30,000** · upfront **1,000** · annual fees **6,000**.
- **Retained Earnings Calculator (`/tools/retained-earnings-calculator`):** Live client-side period-RE tool (`componentKey: 'RetainedEarningsCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e990143`, `nameKey` `retained earnings calculator`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): Dividends distributed (earnings / payout % → dividends) + Retained earnings (RE / shares → REPS); payout &gt;100% allowed with warning; zero/negative earnings do not infer dividends; period RE ≠ cumulative RE. Denomination-only CurrencySelect (PKR default, no FX). No beginning-RE / direct-dividend UI (ADR-023). Activated; **`indexable` left false**. Example: NI **$1M** · payout **30%** · shares **50k** → Div **$300k** · RE **$700k** · REPS **$14**.

Screenshot-first (ADR-023): Net credit sales / Accounts opening / Accounts closing → Average AR / Receivables turnover (times); average AR independent of sales; zero avg AR → undefined (not Infinity); zero sales → 0×; expandable breakdown. Screenshot-first (ADR-023): P1·Q1·P2·Q2 → midpoint (arc) PED; signed coefficient + |PED| classification; initial/final revenue; revenue % change (undefined at zero R₁); equal-price / zero-avg-Q guards; positive-PED caution. Example: $50→$60 · 200→150 → PED **−1.5714** (elastic) · revenue **−10%**.
- **Optimal Price Calculator (`/tools/optimal-price-calculator`):** Live client-side pricing tool (`componentKey: 'OptimalPriceCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e99013f`, `nameKey` `optimal price calculator`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): MC / optional MR / P1·Q1·P2·Q2 → constant-elasticity ε = ln(Q2/Q1)/ln(P2/P1); P* = MC×ε/(ε+1) only when MC>0 and ε&lt;−1; Q* = Q₀(P*/P₀)^ε; profits at initial/final/optimal (zero FC); midpoint PED descriptive; MR diagnostic vs implied MR; no finite optimum for unit/inelastic/positive ε or MC=0. Denomination-only CurrencySelect (PKR default, no FX). No linear-demand / constraints / chart UI (ADR-023). Activated; **`indexable` left false**. Example: MC $40 · 100@$100 → 64@$125 → ε **−2** · P* **$80** · Q* **156.25** · π* **$6,250**.

- Express server bootstrap, `/api/v1/health`, Zod env config, Mongoose connection & graceful shutdown (`backend/src/server.ts`, `app.ts`). After Mongo connects, a currency job is armed for the next local midnight.
- Admin authentication: Argon2id hashing, MongoDB TTL `AdminSession`, `HttpOnly` `admin_session` cookie, Edge + Server layout route protection, brute-force lockout, IP rate limiters, SMTP password reset, profile name update, password change with global session revocation (`backend/src/modules/auth/`, `frontend/src/server/auth/`).
- Admin Category tree management (`/admin/categories`, `new`, `[id]/edit`) — 3-level `category → pillar → sub_pillar` CRUD, search, pagination, cascade delete, and `categories:seed` script (`19` categories, `155` pillars). Root categories also store `iconUrl` / `iconMedia` (`categories:icons`) and a public `description` (`categories:descriptions` fills empty root descriptions). The create and edit forms save that description.
- Admin Media Library (`/admin/media`) — `STORAGE_DRIVER=local` stores files in `backend/uploads` (served at `/uploads`). `STORAGE_DRIVER=s3` keeps the signed browser `PUT`. MongoDB `MediaAsset` records the URL. List, filter, preview, and delete work for both.
- Admin Tool Catalog CRUD (`/admin/tools`, `new`, `[id]/edit`) — full catalog metadata, placement validation, icon selection from media library, `executionType` + allowlisted `componentKey`, SEO fields, `tools:seed` (`4,351` tools seeded inactive/non-indexable), `tools:publish` (15 active browser tools per category, 20 featured), and sole reusable `tool:assign` CLI (`assign-tool-component.ts`; ADR-024 — no per-tool assign scripts).
- Public Catalog API: `/api/v1/catalog/categories`, `/api/v1/catalog/featured`, and `/api/v1/catalog/tools/:slug` (includes `relatedTools` from existing MongoDB category/pillar siblings).
- Homepage `/` Server Component integration linking featured tools and categories.
- Public About page (`/about`): redesigned editorial layout. Copy is static. Category and tool totals are read from `/catalog/categories` and hidden if that request fails.

Currency change converts mortgage balance/fees/other money amounts; rates/term/ARM ratios unchanged. Missing rates block conversion; stale rates warn. Example: 250,000 / 20y / 5% / 10/1 / +0.5% / 8% → ≈ 1,649.89 – 1,831.07 payment range, APR 6.125% (actuarial 5.357%), interest 162,983.32, total 412,983.32. Activated.

- **100 Day Calculator (`/tools/100-day-calculator`):** Live client-side calendar calculator (`componentKey: 'HundredDayCalculator'`). Add or subtract 1–100,000 days, or count signed calendar days between two dates. Inclusive offset treats the start as day 1 (target is N−1 days away). Inclusive span counts both endpoints (same day = 1; exclusive same day = 0). Dates are `YYYY-MM-DD` civil dates added in UTC, so daylight saving time cannot shift the day. Years 1–9999. Shared master shell, SEO, ads, and embed. Unit tests in `hundred-day/calculation.test.ts`. January 1, 2026 + 100 exclusive days = April 11, 2026.
- **12 Volt Wire Size Calculator (`/tools/12-volt-wire-size-calculator`):** Live client-side two-wire DC voltage-drop estimator (`componentKey: 'TwelveVoltWireCalculator'`). Defaults 12 V, 3% (0.36 V), copper, 10 A, 5 m one way, 50°C. Resistivity is IEC 60228 at 20°C with α = 0.00393 /°C (copper) and 0.00403 /°C (aluminium). Area = 2 × ρ(T) × I × L / Vdrop. AWG areas use ASTM B258. Metric sizes are nominal and are not treated as AWG equivalents. Ampacity is not verified, so the page shows the voltage-drop minimum and does not issue a code-compliant recommendation. Switching metres/feet or °C/°F converts the entered number. Shared shell, SEO, ads, and embed. Tests in `twelve-volt-wire/calculation.test.ts` and `electrical/conductor.test.ts`. Default case minimum area is 5.354 mm²; listed sizes 6 mm² and 9 AWG; loop resistance 0.036 Ω; wiring loss 3.6 W.
- **12-Hour Shift Pay Calculator (`/tools/12-hour-shift-pay-calculator`):** Live client-side pay estimate (`componentKey: 'TwelveHourShiftPayCalculator'`). Screenshot-first simplification (ADR-023): primary inputs are hourly wage, overtime hourly wage, and regular hours only. Shift length is fixed at 12; overtime hours = max(12 − regularHours, 0). Defaults 20 / 30 / 8 → daily 280, average 23.33, week 1,400, month 6,160 (×4.4), year 72,800 (×52). Schedule multipliers are internal constants, not form fields. Regular hours above 12 are rejected (not clamped). Display currency defaults to PKR and changes the symbol only. Shared shell, SEO, ads, and embed. Tests in `twelve-hour-shift-pay/calculation.test.ts`.
- **0-60 Calculator (`/tools/0-60-calculator`):** Live client-side acceleration estimate (`componentKey: 'ZeroToSixtyCalculator'`). Defaults 300 hp, 3,500 lb, RWD, automatic, standard tires, dry, 0% grade, normal launch. Fixed-step integration (5 ms, 30 s cap) from rest to 60 mph (26.8224 m/s) and, separately, 100 km/h. Wheel force is the lesser of traction and `P_wheel / v`, with a 0.92 average-power factor because no torque curve is available. Shift delay, drag, rolling resistance, and grade are included. Default result is 5.98 s to 60 mph and 6.25 s to 100 km/h. Not a manufacturer specification. Shared shell, SEO, ads, and embed. Tests in `zero-to-sixty/calculation.test.ts`.
- **3D Printer Buy vs Outsource Calculator (`/tools/3d-printer-buy-vs-outsource-calculator`):** Live client-side cost comparison (`componentKey: 'ThreeDPrinterBuyVsOutsourceCalculator'`). In-house cost is printer + setup + maintenance − resale, plus material, electricity, and labor on expected attempts. Failed attempts use the same material, time, and labor as a success (`attempts = good parts / (1 − failure rate)`). Outsourcing is parts × (price + finishing) + shipping per order + one service fee. Break-even is reported only when the in-house variable cost per good part is lower and the monthly margin repays ownership. Shipping stays per order. Display currency defaults to PKR and does not convert amounts. Default example: 51,733.33 in-house vs 96,000 outsourced. Tests in `three-d-printer-buy-vs-outsource/calculation.test.ts`.
- **3x Rent Calculator (`/tools/3x-rent-calculator`):** Live client-side rent-to-income screen (`componentKey: 'ThreeXRentCalculator'`). Default mode multiplies monthly rent by 3 to get required gross monthly income, then multiplies by 12 for the annual figure. Affordable-rent mode divides gross monthly income by the same multiplier. Two or more tenants can split the requirement equally or by percents that add to 100%. Individual gross incomes are summed before the affordable-rent limit. Equal shares are an assumption. The 3× multiple is a screening guideline, not a legal rule. Display currency defaults to PKR and does not convert amounts. Example: rent 1,500 at 3× requires 4,500 gross monthly and 54,000 gross annual. Tests in `three-x-rent/calculation.test.ts`.
- **Rent Split Calculator (`/tools/rent-split-calculator`):** Live client-side roommate rent split (`componentKey: 'RentSplitCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ffc1`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): total rent with real Phase 2 FX, bedroom count One/Two/Three, per-bedroom people (`person(s)`). Equal per-person split; bedroom share = (people / total people) × total rent; per-person rent shown. Zero total people → no result. People counts never convert. Share/Reload/Clear/feedback. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Activated.
- **A/R Days Calculator (`/tools/ar-days-calculator`):** Live client-side days-sales-outstanding calculator (`componentKey: 'ARDaysCalculator'`). Screenshot-first (ADR-023): three primary inputs (accounts receivable, net credit sales, days in period default 365) and one inline A/R days result. Formula `(receivables / net credit sales) × days`. Optional collapsed Average balance calculation with beginning/ending A/R, calculated average, and explicit Use average A/R / Use ending A/R. No reverse-solve modes, turnover cards, or result dashboards. Empty money fields until valid input. Display currency defaults to PKR and does not convert amounts. Example: 50,000 / 500,000 × 365 = 36.5 days. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Tests in `ar-days/calculation.test.ts`.
- **ADR Calculator (`/tools/adr-calculator`):** Live client-side hotel average daily rate (`componentKey: 'ADRCalculator'`). ADR = room revenue / sold room-nights. An explicit target can solve revenue or room-nights instead. Fractional solved room-nights are kept and labeled, because a night audit uses whole nights. Available room-nights, or physical rooms × days, are optional and only then produce occupancy and RevPAR. Sold room-nights cannot exceed that inventory. Room revenue excludes food, beverage, spa, and other non-room charges. Display currency defaults to PKR and does not convert amounts. A higher ADR is not treated as automatically better. Example: 150,000 / 100 = 1,500; 160 available room-nights → occupancy 62.5% and RevPAR 937.50. Tests in `adr/calculation.test.ts`.
- **401(k) Calculator (`/tools/401k-calculator`):** Live client-side retirement projection (`componentKey: 'FourZeroOneKCalculator'`). Balance mode projects salary growth, an employee percent or fixed deferral, and one employer formula (none, dollar-for-dollar, partial match, or a fixed percent of salary). Contribution-needed mode binary-searches the employee percent or the starting fixed amount. The period rate is `(1 + annual return) ^ (1 / periods) − 1`. Fees use the same convention. Starting balance + employee deposits + vested employer deposits + growth − fees equals the ending balance in cents. Inflation only deflates the displayed balance. An optional withdrawal rate is a planning sketch, not a tax calculation. Contribution limits stay off until the user types USD figures; no IRS table is stored, and limits are refused when the display currency is not USD. Traditional vs Roth is a label. Display currency defaults to USD and does not convert amounts. Example: salary 80,000, employee 6%, 50% match on the first 6%, one year, no return → employee 4,800, employer 2,400. Tests in `four-zero-one-k/calculation.test.ts`.
- **403(b) Calculator (`/tools/403b-calculator`):** Live client-side retirement projection (`componentKey: 'FourZeroThreeBCalculator'`). Final-balance mode projects salary growth, employee deferrals, and an employer match or fixed percent. Contribution-needed mode binary-searches the employee percent or fixed annual amount. Salary-or-match mode solves one of those unknowns. Default compounding is nominal (annual rate ÷ periods); effective annual compounding is a separate choice and is never mixed in the same run. Fees use the same convention and stay a separate line so starting balance + employee deposits + vested employer deposits + growth − fees equals the ending balance in cents. Inflation only deflates the displayed balance. Contribution limits are off until the user types a deferral cap, an optional catch-up, and an optional annual-additions cap. No IRS dollar schedule is stored. Traditional vs Roth is a label; taxes are not calculated. Display currency defaults to USD and does not convert amounts. Example: salary 60,000, employee 6%, 50% match on the first 6%, one year, no growth → employee 3,600, employer 1,800. Tests in `four-zero-three-b/calculation.test.ts`.
- **70/20/10 Rule Money Calculator (`/tools/702010-rule-money-calculator`):** Live client-side budget split (`componentKey: 'SeventyTwentyTenCalculator'`). Needs, savings and debt, and lifestyle default to 70/20/10 of after-tax income. Custom percentages are used only when selected and only when they add up to 100%. Monthly and annual views convert the entered amounts once. Actual spending is compared with each allocation. A zero allocation does not invent a usage percentage. Display currency defaults to PKR and does not convert amounts. The split is a guideline. Example: monthly after-tax income 100,000 → needs 70,000, savings and debt 20,000, lifestyle 10,000. Tests in `seventy-twenty-ten-rule/calculation.test.ts`.
- **529 Calculator (`/tools/529-calculator`):** Live client-side college savings projection (`componentKey: 'FiveTwentyNineCalculator'`). Monthly rates are the annual percent divided by 12. Education costs inflate from an amount the user enters. Scholarships inflate with that rate, are capped at the year's cost, and are not withdrawn again. The balance at college start is separate from the sum withdrawn during college. The required monthly contribution is a bounded bisection and stays unsolved when college starts immediately and the target is still short. Display currency defaults to USD and does not convert amounts or change US tax treatment. No IRS dollar limit is stored. Default illustration: age 8 to 18, four years, 20,000 annual cost, 5% inflation, 10,000 balance, 250 per month, 6% return, 0.2% fee produces a college-start balance of 58,363.63, a total education cost of 140,414.79, a gap of 80,515.19, and a required monthly contribution of 682.99. Tests in `five-twenty-nine/calculation.test.ts`. Catalog id `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e9900f3`.
- **Amortization Calculator (`/tools/amortization-calculator`):** Live client-side amortization calculator (`componentKey: 'AmortizationCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e990149`, `executionType: 'client'`, `isActive: true`, `isDeveloped: true`, `indexable: true`, `embedEnabled: true`). Features loan specifications (principal, term in years/months, annual nominal APR), compounding frequencies (daily, weekly, biweekly, monthly, quarterly, semi-annually, annually), payment frequencies (weekly, biweekly, monthly, quarterly, semi-annually, annually), recurring extra payments, advanced extra payment windows (start/end periods), and one-time lump-sum principal payments. Numerically stable effective periodic rate and scheduled payment calculation. Pure cents-based amortization ledger with exact zero ending balance reconciliation. Balance trajectory SVG curve with baseline comparison, Principal vs Interest composition breakdown, interactive schedule table with period/yearly views, pagination, CSV export, and full-screen modal. 14 unit test suites pass (`amortization/calculation.test.ts`). Shared Auto Loan master shell, dynamic SEO, dummy ads, and embed supported. Default example: 10,000 at 12.5% for 10 years monthly yields 146.38/mo, 7,565.76 total interest, and 17,565.76 total paid.

- **Height in Inches Calculator (`/tools/height-in-inches-calculator`):** Live client-side height converter (`componentKey: 'HeightInInchesCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f17a27d8d1d4e990b89`). Exact factor `1 acre = 40.468564224 ares` (from 4,046.8564224 m² / 100 m²). Defaults 5 ac = 202.34282112 a. `isDeveloped` set true; `isActive` preserved true; `indexable` and `isFeatured` left false. Tests in `acre-to-are/calculation.test.ts`.

- **Calculator Embed System (`/embed/[slug]`):** Shared iframe surface for allowlisted calculators. **Embedding is enabled by default**; only an explicit admin OFF disables it (`resolveEmbedEnabled`). Public **Embed** button when eligible. CSP allows third-party framing only on `/embed/*`; embed pages are `noindex` with `no-store` cache and no ads. Standards: `brain/EMBED_STANDARDS.md`.
- **Dummy Ad Placeholders (`/tools/[slug]` only):** Reusable `AdSlot` (`tool_top_banner`, `tool_article_middle`, `tool_bottom_banner` on by default; sidebar/article-bottom off). Placeholder mode only — no AdSense. Embeds excluded. Standards: `brain/AD_STANDARDS.md`.
- **Tool Detail Page standards lock (docs):** Authoritative `TOOL_DETAIL_PAGE_STANDARDS.md`, step playbook `NEW_TOOL_DEVELOPMENT_PLAYBOOK.md`, Cursor `.cursor/rules/tool-detail-page-standards.mdc`, Antigravity `.agents/rules/tool-detail-page-standards.md`, and `AGENTS.md` references — master is Auto Loan (verified 2026-11-13).
- **Calculator module lock (docs, ADR-018):** New calculators use `ARCHITECTURE.md` §7: pure `calculation.ts` / `validation.ts`, `detail.tsx`, lazy `registry/index.ts`, and `registry/details.ts`. Mandatory checklist is in the playbook. Page design, SEO, embed, and ads are unchanged. Existing gaps are listed under Known Issues and were not refactored.
- **Two-commit calculator delivery (docs, ADR-019 amended 2026-11-17):** New calculators use exactly two commits — (1) all tool files, (2) Brain docs last — pushed together to `origin/development`. Size-banded milestone sequences are retired. ADR-016 and ADR-018 are unchanged. Cursor `.cursor/rules/git-commits.mdc` and Antigravity `.agents/rules/git-commits.md` apply the rule.
- **Calculator activation (ADR-022 + ADR-023 verified activation):** Registered calculators are normally `isActive` on their existing catalog rows, with embed on (missing `embedEnabled` counts as on). After tests and route verification, set `isDeveloped`, `isActive`, and eligible `indexable`. Exceptions left `isActive: false` per task while `isDeveloped: true`: Ares to Cent Converter, Light Year Conversion, Ares to Hectares Converter, and Gallons to Pounds Converter. Catalog rows without a module stay inactive. Loan and Auto Loan stay `indexable: false`, so they are public but not sitemap-eligible.
- **Currency rates (ADR-017 / ADR-021):** Phase 1 read path (`GET /api/v1/currency/rates`, `getCurrencyRates`) plus Phase 2 shared conversion engine (`convertCurrency`, `useConvertibleMoneyField`, `CurrencyRatesProvider`). Formula `amount * rate[target] / rate[source]`; unrounded canonical `MoneyValue`; round only on display. Calculator modules not wired yet — denomination calculators unchanged. Local API key missing; stored rates stale (2026-11-13). Tracked metadata: 54 codes.
- **Screenshot-First Calculator Simplicity Standard (ADR-023):** Permanent rule for all future calculators. The reference screenshot defines functional scope (WHAT the calculator does); Auto Loan is the visual design master (HOW it looks), not the functional master. Unrequested inputs, advanced drawers, charts, and metric dashboards are prohibited unless essential for mathematical correctness or explicitly requested. Currency selectors are included only when explicitly justified. Results are compact, and schedules/breakdowns stay behind optional disclosure controls.

- **Attrition Rate Calculator (`/tools/attrition-rate-calculator`):** Live client-side workforce attrition calculator (`componentKey: 'AttritionRateCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e99007f`, `executionType: 'client'`, `isActive: true`, `isDeveloped: true`, `indexable: true`, `embedEnabled: true`). Built to strict Screenshot-First Simplicity Standard (ADR-023) matching reference screenshot: 4-field card layout (Employees at start, Employees at end, Employees that left, inline Attrition rate % output). Formula: `Average = (Start + End) / 2`, `Attrition rate (%) = (Left / Average) * 100`. Initially blank inputs; auto-calculates on valid non-negative inputs; allows rates > 100%; no currency dropdown or charts; integrated share result, reload calculator, clear changes, and satisfaction feedback controls. Unit tests in `attrition-rate/calculation.test.ts`.

- **Business Loan Calculator (`/tools/business-loan-calculator`):** Live client-side commercial business loan calculator (`componentKey: 'BusinessLoanCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff4f`, `executionType: 'client'`, `isActive: true`, `isDeveloped: true`, `indexable: true`, `embedEnabled: true`). Implemented strictly adhering to Screenshot-First Simplicity Standard (ADR-023). Exact 3-section layout: (1) Main specifications (known rate dropdown, interest rate %, compound frequency, loan amount with PKR denomination currency, term in years/months, payment frequency); (2) Additional fees (prepaid fee, loaned fee, origination fee %, origination fee repayment modes: from loan amount, rolling into loan, out of pocket); (3) Compact results (informational callout, net loan received, periodic payment, total interest payment, total payment, total finance charge, APR, effective APR). Accurate amortization and actuarial APR solving. Unit tests in `business-loan/calculation.test.ts`.

- **Car Interest Calculator (`/tools/car-interest-calculator`):** Live client-side auto-loan interest (`componentKey: 'CarInterestCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff7f`). Two sections: Calculate the loan amount (car price / down payment / trade-in) + Car interest (calculated loan amount, rate %, yrs/mos term, monthly payment, total interest). Formulas: P = price − down − trade-in; n = years×12+months; r = APR/1200; M = P×r/(1−(1+r)^(−n)) (or P/n if r=0); interest = M×n−P. Independent real FX on all six money fields; mixed inputs normalize into loan-amount currency. Example: 2,000,000 − 400,000 − 100,000 @ 12% / 5y → P 1,500,000; payment ≈ 33,366.67; interest ≈ 502,000.29. Activated. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.

Fields: car price, money you have, trade-in, sales tax %, loan amount, interest rate, term yrs/mos, monthly payment, total interest. Formulas: Tax = Price×%/100; Loan = Price+Tax−Money−Trade-in; n=12Y+M; r=APR/1200; Payment=P×r/(1−(1+r)^(-n)) (P/n if r=0); Interest=Payment×n−P. Independent real FX on six money fields; normalize into loan-amount currency. Example: 2,000,000 − 400,000 − 100,000 @ 12% / 5y → P 1,500,000; payment ≈ 33,366.67; interest ≈ 502,000.29. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Activated.

Example 100,000 PKR / 6% / 10 yrs / period 3 yrs → ≈ 75,996.91 remaining. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on.
- **Partially Amortized Loan Calculator (`/tools/partially-amortized-loan-calculator`):** Live client-side balloon loan tool (`componentKey: 'PartiallyAmortizedLoanCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff5b`). Full loan, rate %/yr, amort time N, payment period K → monthly payment (from N), total paid (M×K), balloon after K, total. Independent FX on five money fields. Example 100,000 / 6% / 20y amort / 5y pay → M ≈ 716.43, balloon ≈ 84,899.60. Activated.
- **Payday Loan Calculator (`/tools/payday-loan-calculator`):** Live client-side payday APR tool (`componentKey: 'PaydayLoanCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff5d`). Loan amount, finance charge, term days, APR %, APR amount, monthly fee %, monthly fee, total debt. Simple APR `(F/P)×(365/d)×100` with bidirectional F↔APR. APR amount = annualized F. Monthly fee = one `P×(q/100)` assessment. Total debt = P+F+fee. Independent FX on five money fields. Example 1,000 / 150 / 14d → APR ≈ 391.07%, total 1,150. Activated.
- **Dividend Discount Model Calculator (`/tools/dividend-discount-model-calculator`):** Live client-side Gordon Growth DDM (`componentKey: 'DividendDiscountModelCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e990005`). Four sections: expected growth, expected dividend, CAPM cost of equity, DDM stock value. Independent FX on D₀ / D₁ / P₀ (Margin Interest pattern). Defaults: payout 40%, ROE 15%, D₀ 5 PKR, Rf 4%, MRP 6%, β 1.2 → g 9%, D₁ 5.45, Ke 11.2%, P₀ ≈ 247.73. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on.
- **Cost of Equity Calculator (`/tools/cost-of-equity-calculator`):** Live client-side dividend-growth cost of equity (`componentKey: 'CostOfEquityCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e990154`). Yes/No dividend radio (Yes default). Yes mode: Dividend per share as D₁, current share price, growth rate % → cost of equity % via `(D₁/P₀)+g`. Independent FX selectors on dividend and share price (ADR-021); amounts convert on currency change and normalize into share-price currency before the ratio; growth % and result % never convert. Zero growth allowed; share price &gt; 0; non-negative dividend. No mode: switchable but CAPM fields not invented (reference screenshot omitted them). Omega UI. Activated. Example: D₁ 5 / P₀ 100 / g 4% → 9%; mixed 10 USD / 2,770 PKR / g 3% @ 277 → 103%. Tests in `cost-of-equity/calculation.test.ts` (not run this delivery).
- **Cash Back or Low Interest Calculator (`/tools/cash-back-or-low-interest-calculator`):** Live client-side auto-offer comparison (`componentKey: 'CashBackOrLowInterestCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff81`). Cash-back amount + high APR vs low APR; shared auto price, term months, down, trade-in, sales tax %, title/registration fees, optional finance-all-fees. Fixed USD (no currency selector). State dropdown lists verified US states/DC only — never invents or overwrites tax rates. Compares total economic cost (price + interest + tax + fees − cash back once). Example: $50k / $1k @ 5% vs 2% / 60 mo / $10k down / 7% tax / $2k fees upfront → cash-back ≈ $735.98/mo · $59,658.69 total; low-rate ≈ $701.11/mo · $57,566.62 total (low rate better; interest savings ≈ $3,092). Activated. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Carried Interest Calculator (`/tools/carried-interest-calculator`):** Live client-side PE carry tool (`componentKey: 'CarriedInterestCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff80`). Fund return section (initial/final → fund return %) + carry interest (hold years default 10, hurdle default 0%, carry default 20%, GP catch-up No/Yes). Without catch-up: carry × max(0, profit − compounded hurdle). With Yes: standard full catch-up waterfall (no extra fields; not verified against an expanded Omni Yes-mode screenshot). Compact GP carry amount result. Real FX: two synced selectors convert initial+final together via `convertDraftMoneyFields` / stored rates (`CURRENCY_CONVERSION_SLUGS`); return %, hurdle %, carry %, and hold period unchanged by FX-only changes. Omega UI. Activated. Example: 100,000→150,000 / 10y / 0% / 20% / No → return 50%, profit 50,000, GP carry 10,000. Tests in `carried-interest/calculation.test.ts` (not run this delivery).
- **Sinking Fund Calculator (`/tools/sinking-fund-calculator`):** Live client-side USSF tool (`componentKey: 'SinkingFundCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e9900e9`). Money to accumulate, annual interest rate %, compounding frequency (default Yearly), period years/months (default 5y 0m) → USSF factor and required contribution. USSF = i/((1+i)^n−1); zero rate → 1/n; contribution = FV×USSF at end of each compounding period. Months = months/12 (n = m×t may be fractional). Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: 100,000 @ 5% yearly × 5y → USSF ≈ 0.180975, contribution ≈ 18,097.48. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Perpetuity Calculator (`/tools/perpetuity-calculator`):** Live client-side Gordon Growth / growing-perpetuity valuation (`componentKey: 'PerpetuityCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e9900e3`). Dividend (D₁), discount rate %, growth rate % (default 0); inline present value = Dividend / (discount − growth). Discount must exceed growth; zero and negative growth allowed when valid; non-negative dividend. Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: 1,000 / 10% / 0% → 10,000; 1,000 / 10% / 4% → ≈ 16,666.67. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Present Value Calculator (`/tools/present-value-calculator`):** Live client-side discounting tool (`componentKey: 'PresentValueCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e9900e4`). Future value, whole-number periods, per-period interest rate % → present value and total interest (FV − PV). Rate greater than -100%; non-negative FV; zero rate/periods allowed. Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: 12,100 / 2 / 10% → PV 10,000, interest 2,100. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Rate of Return Calculator (`/tools/rate-of-return-calculator`):** Live client-side annual nominal rate solver (`componentKey: 'RateOfReturnCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e990013`). Main specs (initial investment, final amount, investment length yrs/mos, compounding) + interim cash flows (deposit/withdrawal, frequency, end/beginning timing); statement result. Closed form without PMT; bisection with PMT. Distinct from Annualized Return / Annualized Rate of Return / Percentage Return. Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: 1,000 → 5,000 / 10y / monthly / PMT 0 → ≈ 16.203%. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Time Value of Money Calculator (`/tools/time-value-of-money-calculator`):** Live client-side bidirectional PV ↔ FV tool (`componentKey: 'TimeValueOfMoneyCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e9900eb`). Interest rate %, term years/months, compound frequency (default Yearly); last-edited money field drives the other. FV = PV × (1+r/m)^(m×t); PV = FV / (1+r/m)^(m×t). Distinct from Present Value and Future Value calculators. Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: 10% / 2y / yearly / PV 10,000 → FV 12,100. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Savings Interest Rate Calculator (`/tools/savings-interest-rate-calculator`):** Live client-side rate solver (`componentKey: 'SavingsInterestRateCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e98ff90`). Goal, initial balance, term years, compounding, deposit amount/frequency/timing → required annual nominal rate %, total deposited, yearly principal/interest/balance chart. Closed-form annuity + bisection; fixed PKR (no currency selector). Distinct from Savings / Interest Rate / Investment calculators. Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on. Example: goal 100,000 / P 0 / 30y / monthly / PMT 100 end → ≈ 5.976%, deposited 36,000. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.
- **Real Rate of Return Calculator (`/tools/real-rate-of-return-calculator`):** Live client-side Fisher real return (`componentKey: 'RealRateOfReturnCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e990017`). Nominal rate %, inflation rate % → real rate %. Formula ((1+n/100)/(1+i/100)−1)×100. Nominal ≥ −100%; inflation > −100%; no currency. Omega UI. Activated. Example: 10% / 4% → ≈ 5.7692%. Lint/builds/browser/automated tests omitted per user.

### Partially Implemented / UI Preview Only
- **Public Directory (`/tools`, `/categories`, `/categories/[slug]`):** `/tools` lists active tools from `GET /catalog/tools`, 24 per page, with name search and a category filter. `/categories` and `/categories/[slug]` read the live catalog. A category page lists active tools only, grouped by pillar and sub-pillar. Header, footer, and the mobile menu use the same live category list. `/tools/[slug]` returns 404 unless the active tool's `componentKey` is registered. Each registered tool's guide lives in its own `detail.tsx`, and the calculator loads with `import()`. `frontend/src/lib/sample-data.ts` is removed.
- **Other Tool Implementations (`frontend/src/tools/`):** Registered calculators continue through `AbsencePercentageCalculator` (and peer tools such as Accumulated Depreciation / AFN). Ares to Cent, Light Year Conversion, Ares to Hectares, and Gallons to Pounds stay catalog-inactive (`isActive: false`) until published. Remaining category folders and other catalog tools stay unimplemented.
- **Public Contact (`/contact`):** Redesigned page (`ece3bce`). The form validates name, email, subject (`general inquiry`, `feature request`, `bug report`, `partnership`, `other`), and message in the browser, then explicitly states that the message was not delivered; no backend email/ticket endpoint exists.
- **Legal Pages (`/privacy-policy`, `/terms-of-service`):** Draft templates pending legal review.
- **Admin Preview Pages (`/admin`, `/admin/analytics`, `/admin/activity-logs`, `/admin/integrations`, `/admin/pages`, `/admin/seo`, `/admin/settings`, `/admin/users`):** Complete UI rendered from `frontend/src/lib/admin-mock-data.ts` with `PendingNotice` banners; backend endpoints/models do not exist yet.
- **Admin Profile 2FA Switch (`AdminProfileClient.tsx`):** UI toggle state only; no TOTP backend exists.

### Pending / Planned
- **Auto Loan Calculator financial calculation accuracy testing** (dedicated QA phase before marking math formulas sign-off complete).
- Wiring is done for public `/tools`, `/categories`, and `/categories/[slug]`. `listFeaturedTools` still does not require `isActive: true`.
- Building remaining client-side/server-side tool components under `frontend/src/tools/` (`TOOL-01`) and registering them.
- Backend modules currently containing `.gitkeep` only: `analytics`, `integrations`, `jobs`, `seo`, `uploads`, `users`.
- Attaching `requireRole` middleware to admin routes and implementing staff management.

## 3. Known Issues & Drift

1. **Catalog Featured Filter:** `catalog.service.ts` `listFeaturedTools()` queries `{ isFeatured: true }` without checking `isActive: true`.
2. **Env Var Drift:** `backend/.env.example` lists `SESSION_TTL_HOURS`/`SESSION_REMEMBER_ME_DAYS` and `frontend/.env.example` lists `ADMIN_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME`/`COOKIE_SECURE`, which are currently hardcoded in `admin-auth.service.ts` (`24h`/`7d`) and `session.ts` (`'admin_session'`, `NODE_ENV === 'production'`).
3. **Residual Branding Strings:** `mega_tools` DB default, `megatools.io` placeholders, and `mega-tools/platform` link remain in non-critical defaults/UI previews.
4. **Calculator module gaps (left in place):** `hundred-day` has no `types.ts` (types are in `calculation.ts`). `StickyCalculatorAside`, `TableOfContents`, and `InfoTooltip` still live under `auto-loan/` and are imported by the shared page. `APPROVED_COMPONENT_KEYS` is duplicated in frontend `registry/keys.ts` and backend `tool.key.ts`.

Next priorities: formal financial QA on Auto Loan Calculator, `listFeaturedTools` active filter (`BE-02`), and subsequent tools via the playbook (`TOOL-01`).

- **AFFO Calculator (`/tools/affo-calculator`):** Live simplified REIT bridge (`AFFOCalculator`, catalog `6af46f10a27d8d1d4e990111`). FFO adds eligible real-estate depreciation and property-sale losses and subtracts eligible property-sale gains. Interest income is not applied again. Simplified AFFO subtracts straight-line rent, recurring maintenance capex, and incremental maintenance not already in FFO. Optional lines cover noncash compensation, debt-issuance amortization, and signed lease amortization. Not Nareit FFO. Example: FFO 1,370,000 and AFFO 1,245,000.

- **Profitability Index Calculator (`/tools/profitability-index-calculator`):** Live client-side PI calculator (`componentKey: 'ProfitabilityIndexCalculator'`, catalog `6af46f0fa27d8d1d4e9900b2`). Initial investment + PV of future cash flow → PI = PV ÷ investment; compact interpretation (PI >, =, < 1). Optional “PV unknown” tip (no invented cash-flow schedule). Denomination currency (PKR). Omega UI. `isDeveloped`/`isActive`/`indexable` true; embed on.
